It controls epistemic status and containment, never evidence visibility or presumptive acceptance/rejection.","legacy_docket_assessment":{"adjudicable":false,"packet":null,"status":"invalid"},"packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity":[{"focus_ref":"93:8","identity_status":"legacy_unbound","packet_identity":null,"protocol":"focus-trace-hermetic-response-v4","reader_id":"reader_hft_a","trace_kind":"reconstructed"}]},"reader_synthesis_count":9,"source_present":true,"structured_insight_count":15,"unstructured_record_count":0},"identity":{"ayah_ref":"93:8","lane":"global","linguistic_source_ref":"93:8","surface_ref":"93:8","unit_kind":"numbered_ayah"},"primary_floor":{"source_ref":"93:8","target_tokens":[["Seni",["93:8:1"]],["yoksul",["93:8:2"]],["buldu",["93:8:1"]],["ve",["93:8:3"]],["zenginleştirdi",["93:8:3"]]],"text":"Seni yoksul buldu ve zenginleştirdi."},"schema_version":"commentary-v5-hermetic-scope-packet-v1","scope":{"hft":{"assigned_record_count":9,"authoring_policy":{"every_assigned_record_requires_explicit_review":true,"parseable_hft_is_always_visible_to_authoring":true,"provenance_affects_qualification_not_visibility":true,"scope_mismatch_affects_lane_assignment_not_visibility":true,"unresolved_branch_citations_do_not_suppress_records":true},"authoring_status":"visible_with_provenance_qualification"},"lane_contract":"reader walks and cross-run publications with explicit trust labels","pericope":{"ayah_from":1,"ayah_to":11,"id":"s093-p01-001-011","label":"Whole surah","number":1,"refs":["93:1","93:2","93:3","93:4","93:5","93:6","93:7","93:8","93:9","93:10","93:11"]},"readiness":{"authoring_effect":"Docket readiness is reported for source provenance. HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B008","root_001110/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0ccf768c41a79d714ae6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:8:2\",\"root_001068\",[\"B008\"]],[\"93:8:3\",\"root_001110\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yönünü şaşırmış başıboş hayvan ile yerleşik konut çağrışımları, zenginleşmeye barınma ve yön bulmanın ardından kalıcı bir yere yerleşme boyutu ekler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2d4ed119e54c29c1608b","text":"93:9–10 changed the enrichment clause most strongly: orphan and asker made fixed `ع ي ل:B002` and `غ ن ي:B002` outward-facing, so sufficiency became ability to carry claims and stand in for absent support. 93:11 then named the whole process as reportable favor, correcting any reduction of `فَأَغْنَىٰ` to private accumulation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_604313d3043a08b0c482","text":"3. **Stray confusion resolved by sufficing place.** Evidence: ع ي ل B008 (stray animal losing direction), ض ل ل B005 in 93:7 (stray animal in wasteland), غ ن ي B004 (settled dwelling/inhabiting), ء و ي B001 in 93:6 (shelter/refuge). Structural cue: the previous ayah names lostness and guidance; this one names need and enrichment. Mechanism: the packet supplies stray directionlessness and settled dwelling; I supply the move that sufficiency includes being placed somewhere one can dwell. Abductive move: lack is spatial as well as financial: the needy one has no stable ground. Reading change: \"اغنى\" becomes settling and making at-home, not only giving wealth.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B001","root_001110/B001","root_001110/B002","root_001626/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_811a7a2c53f628150ebf","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:8:1\",\"root_001626\",[\"B003\"]],[\"93:8:2\",\"root_001068\",[\"B001\"]],[\"93:8:3\",\"root_001110\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Zenginleştirme, lüks vermekten çok, belirlenen eksikliği giderip kişiyi yeterli ve iş görebilir hale getiren bir kapasite oluşturur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8790b5a247b66084ccac","text":"5. **Voice/modulated gratitude.** Evidence: غ ن ي B003 (song/vocal modulation), ح د ث B003 in 93:11 (speech/report), ن ع م B013 (eye-gladdening courtesy formula), س م و B008 (good repute spread). Structural cue: enrichment is followed by commanded narration of blessing. Mechanism: the packet supplies vocal richness and speech/report branches; I supply the expressive arrow that sufficiency overflows into a voiced account. Abductive move: being made sufficient produces a voice capable of telling favor beautifully. Reading change: \"اغنى\" can activate not only wealth but a voice enriched enough to narrate.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_89de3b6b7c8f9f5e8c9a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:8:3\",\"root_001110\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Zenginliğin ses ve ezgi boyutu, sonraki nimeti anlatma buyruğuyla, yeterliliğin şükrü dile getirebilen bir sese taşmasını düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_96eea7da8a50b9f253af","text":"1. **Finding becomes an assessment and creation of capacity.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ع ي ل:B001` is poverty and need, fixed `غ ن ي:B001` is self-sufficiency and wealth, and fixed `و ج د:B003` is capacity, means, and wealth. **Structural cue:** the finding-root carries a latent means-branch before the clause explicitly reverses need through `فَأَغْنَىٰ`. **Assigned role:** `ع ي ل` diagnoses deficit; the dormant `و ج د` branch anticipates capacity; `غ ن ي` realizes it. **Abductive move:** I infer root-level resonance inside the clause, finding a condition → answering it with means; the packet supplies the branch inventory and syntax but not that internal echo explicitly. **Reading change:** `وَوَجَدَكَ` can involve discerning the precise lack and making capacity present, not merely encountering someone already classified as poor.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9abc0178c2f50cf349cb","text":"2. **Dependent-care reversal.** Evidence: ع ي ل B002 (dependents under support), ر ب ب B005 (fostered child/caregiver), ي ت م B001 in 93:6/9 (orphaned child), س ء ل B001 in 93:10 (requester). Structural cue: the addressee's own dependent/vulnerable state precedes commands about dependent others. Mechanism: the packet supplies dependency branches on both sides; I supply the ethical reversal that one who was supported must not crush or rebuke others needing support. Abductive move: remembered dependency becomes the mechanism for social law. Reading change: 93:8 is not only personal enrichment; it prepares the addressee to become a non-oppressive support node.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B001","root_001626/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9bfed9042a4853db6c2a","text":"1. **Poverty repaired as sufficiency.** Evidence: و ج د B001/B003 (finding; means/wealth), ع ي ل B001 (poverty and need), غ ن ي B001/B002 (wealth/self-sufficiency; sufficing/availing), ر ب ب B002 (nurture/completion). Structural cue: this is the third find → lack → remedy line. Mechanism: the packet supplies need and sufficiency branches; I supply the completion arrow from exposed lack to enoughness. Abductive move: enrichment means making the found state viable, not necessarily luxury. Reading change: \"made rich\" reads as making sufficient and capable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B005","root_001583/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a93d19493da704034f25","text":"4. **Marriage/protection as sufficiency, exploratory.** Evidence: ع ي ل B005 (woman lacking husband/remains unmarried) indirectly echoed by dependence, غ ن ي B005/B006 (woman made independent by husband/beauty; marriage as protection), ه د ي B006 in 93:7 (bride led to husband). Structural cue: the packet's guidance and sufficiency inventories contain marital-protection branches adjacent to عائل/أغنى. Mechanism: the packet supplies marriage-as-sufficiency branches; I supply the social analogy that a protector relation can convert exposure into sufficiency. Abductive move: the verse's enrichment may include incorporation into protective relations, not merely property. Reading change: this remains exploratory but preserves a household-protection reading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B005","root_001110/B005","root_001110/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b851417d8df5b774a26f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:8:2\",\"root_001068\",[\"B005\"]],[\"93:8:3\",\"root_001110\",[\"B005\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Evlilik yoluyla korunma ve bağımsızlaşma çağrışımları, yeterliliği koruyucu bir ilişkiye dahil olma şeklinde okumayı önerir; bağlam bu özel ilişkiyi doğrulamaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ba3b3f3a041638158239","text":"2. **Sufficiency is capacity to carry dependents.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ع ي ل:B002` is dependents under one's support, and fixed `غ ن ي:B002` is sufficing, availing, or standing in for another. **Structural cue:** 93:9–10 immediately bring an orphan and an asker into view after the enrichment clause. **Assigned role:** the dependents branch enlarges the initial burden; the sufficing branch turns enrichment into ability to support or stand in. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies increased capacity followed by vulnerable claimants; I infer capacity → support of others. **Reading change:** `عَآئِلًا فَأَغْنَىٰ` can move from being unable to carry needs to being made able to sustain them, not only from low to high personal wealth.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e3b4080764e71f7ea474","text":"93:9-10 changed the enrichment reading sharply: اغنى is not a private move from poverty to comfort, because the next two ayat place orphan and requester in front of the now-sufficed addressee. 93:11 then activated غ ن ي B003: sufficiency unexpectedly becomes vocal capacity, a prepared voice for favor.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001068/B002","root_001110/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_eb4f3c157714d8b091fa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:8:2\",\"root_001068\",[\"B002\"]],[\"93:8:3\",\"root_001110\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bakmakla yükümlü olunanlar ve başkasına yetebilme boyutları, kazanılan yeterliliği yetim ve isteyen gibi başkalarının yükünü taşıyabilme gücüne çevirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000070/B001","root_001068/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f60e162e2fc2f7d98787","text":"3. **Enrichment also stabilizes orientation and dwelling.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ع ي ل:B008` is a stray animal unsure of direction, and fixed `غ ن ي:B004` is settled dwelling; 93:6 activates shelter through `ء و ي:B001`, and 93:7 activates direction through `ه د ي:B002`. **Structural cue:** shelter, guidance, and enrichment form three consecutive remedies to three found conditions. **Assigned role:** the stray branch combines dependence with disorientation; settled dwelling supplies durable placement after shelter and guidance. **Abductive move:** I infer the three remedies cooperate, shelter + course + sufficiency → stable habitation; the plus-sign relation is inferred, not stated. **Reading change:** enrichment can include being established in a viable place and course, not only acquisition of property.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The 93:6 parallel changes private wealth into repair of a severed support network and capacity to sustain dependents.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/0","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_81e196d44de131522be8","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
